title:PvE Podcast 174 - The Best Machine Guns
description:Monument of Triumph new and reprised machine guns, based on trait perk discussions between @ImpetusAlways and @Saint_Kabr in the 7 July 2026 @PodvsEnemies podcast (https://open.spotify.com/episode/24DHDdLVFrP15vymMXrHrk). Destiny Data Compendium, Aegis's Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​ and other resources, available at https://www.destiny2.science/, or through https://www.youtube.com/@TheAegisRelic. The effect of weapon perks on stats can be found at https://destiny.report or https://d2foundry.gg.

// Archon's Thunder - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rimestealer), (Headstone)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Archon's Thunder is a high-impact LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "highest total damage without losing that much DPS, also stagger intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just to the right of vertical and pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are the best available. Archon's Thunder can be used for add clear with Stasis builds, perhaps supporting a double special loadout. For that, Rimestealer generates ×2 stacks of Stasis Frost Armor on shattering a Stasis crystal or killing a frozen enemy. In the second column, Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill - these are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc Stasis subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Skulking Wolf origin trait as A-Tier in PvE - after you get a kill when ≤90 HP, it enhances radar and makes adds less accurate when firing at you for 10s, as though you were Amplified. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Qua Vinctus IV - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Hatchling), (Meganeura, Tear)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Strand builds. Qua Vinctus IV is a high-impact LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "highest total damage without losing that much DPS, also stagger intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just to the right of vertical but pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are the best available. To clear adds, perhaps supporting a double special loadout, in the first trait perk column Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, Meganeura builds up to ×3 stacks on crit hits for a refreshable 4s for AoE explosive damage in a 7.5m radius on a crit kill - the explosion is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts and also procs subclass fragments, or Tear spreads ~4m AoE Strand Sever on a crit kill to reduce damage output to ×0.6 from enemies affected. Aegis ranks the Wild Card origin trait as C-Tier - it provides the chance of Telesto-like "Experimental Submunitions" dropping on a kill - the number depends on the enemy tier - that last up to 10.5s and explode on contact or proximity to an enemy for a bit of damage. That's still better than the D-Tier Roar of Battle which The Roar of Battle origin trait grants a barely noticeable +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s. If you're interested, the name translates to "as a memorial" or "for a memory/ remembrance", and links to the ghost Targe reflecting on his bond with Zavala. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Thermal Erosion - PvE Utility god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Incandescent), (Burning Ambition)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE utility roll for solar Warlocks running Eunoia or Dawn Chorus in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Thermal Erosion is a rapid fire LMG, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "good damage profile but mediocre total damage and worst recoil of any LMG frame". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right and loose so you should improve recoil control or look to boost Stability wit your barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. For weapon-based application and progression of scorch into ignitions, you can roll both solar damage perk traits - Incandescent spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+45 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars), with scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments, while Burning Ambition works better to build Solar ignitions on tougher targets if you have a solar ability like a Eunoia Helion to apply scorch initially, otherwise it'll take 27 hits within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks with +20 added on each additional hit to build towards an ignition. All of the scorch stacks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes, and Dawn Chorus provides ×3 Scorch damage. Aegis ranks the Winterized Gear origin trait as F-Tier - it provides ×3 stacks after 12s out of combat - so if you're playing you'll probably never proc it - each stack decays after 5s in combat providing a gradually reducing boost to Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, thermal erosion is the melting and weakening of ice-rich permafrost caused by moving water or temperature changes, and connects to the planetary environment of Europa, the elemental nature of the weapon, and the broader narrative themes of the Beyond Light expansion. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Bitter End - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Ionized Heatsink, Enhanced Heatsink, Accelerated Heatsink, Light Heatsink, Tactical Heatsink), (Trickle Charge, Cooling Baubles), (Jolting Feedback, Rolling Storm)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. Bitter End is a Balanced Heat frame LMG, with a rapid-fire firing rate which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "good damage profile but mediocre total damage and worst recoil of any LMG frame". The heat frame offers built in Anti-Overload, and is like an extra perk set, first providing reduced heat generation, significantly increasing the number of shots before overheating, and providing a pseudo-Reconstruction, slowly "refilling" as the weapon loses heat when not being used. Additionally, there's an intrinsic ×1.15 damage boost against orange and yellow bars and vehicles, plus a ×2 vent rate if you manually reload when the heat bar is at >87.5% of maximum heat to proc Perfect Vent, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just left of vertical and pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the heatsink perk slot, perks that reduce Heat Generation or Vent Speed work best for this roll. Bitter End can be used for boss damage, particularly for Arc builds. If you're behind a Storm's Keep rally barricade, Trickle Charge reduces 10% heat on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target. Otherwise, if someone on your fireteam is producing power orbs, or you can generate Ionic Traces, Cooling Baubles reduces weapon heat by 33% for each Elemental pickup or power orb collected. In the second column for both target and AoE damage, Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 14 hits (11 when amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits, or Rolling Storm generates ×2 stacks of Arc Bolt Charge on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks. Aegis ranks the Imperial Allegiance origin trait as A-Tier - used in a fireteam, it stacks up to ×3 per ally within 15m, boosting cooling by a max ×1.105 and increasing damage when above 50% heat by max ×1.105. If you're interested, this is modelled on on the Z-6 rotary blaster cannon used by Hevy in the Clone Wars series. The name "bitter end" comes from old sailing ship terminology. The "bitts" are the heavy wooden or iron posts on the deck used to secure cables and ropes. The very last section of an anchor rope attached to the bitts is called the "bitter end." So, when an anchor rope was let out all the way to its absolute limit, a sailor reached the bitter end - meaning there was no rope left to give. It's also thought by the community to be a subtle linguistic joke: you start with "Sweet Business," and you finish with the "Bitter End". Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Bitter End - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Ionized Heatsink, Enhanced Heatsink, Accelerated Heatsink, Light Heatsink, Tactical Heatsink), (Trickle Charge, Cooling Baubles), (Killing Tally, One For All)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. Bitter End is a Balanced Heat frame LMG, with a rapid-fire firing rate which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "good damage profile but mediocre total damage and worst recoil of any LMG frame". The heat frame offers built in Anti-Overload, and is like an extra perk set, first providing reduced heat generation, significantly increasing the number of shots before overheating, and providing a pseudo-Reconstruction, slowly "refilling" as the weapon loses heat when not being used. Additionally, there's an intrinsic ×1.15 damage boost against orange and yellow bars and vehicles, plus a ×2 vent rate if you manually reload when the heat bar is at >87.5% of maximum heat to proc Perfect Vent, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just left of vertical and pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the heatsink perk slot, perks that reduce Heat Generation or Vent Speed work best for this roll. Bitter End can be used for boss damage, particularly for Arc builds. If you're behind a Storm's Keep rally barricade, Trickle Charge reduces 10% heat on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target. Otherwise, if someone on your fireteam is producing power orbs, or you can generate Ionic Traces, Cooling Baubles reduces weapon heat by 33% for each Elemental pickup or power orb collected. For damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, or One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each. The Aegis ranks the Imperial Allegiance origin trait as A-Tier - used in a fireteam, it stacks up to ×3 per ally within 15m, boosting cooling by a max ×1.105 and increasing damage when above 50% heat by max ×1.105. If you're interested, this is modelled on on the Z-6 rotary blaster cannon used by Hevy in the Clone Wars series. The name "bitter end" comes from old sailing ship terminology. The "bitts" are the heavy wooden or iron posts on the deck used to secure cables and ropes. The very last section of an anchor rope attached to the bitts is called the "bitter end." So, when an anchor rope was let out all the way to its absolute limit, a sailor reached the bitter end - meaning there was no rope left to give. It's also thought by the community to be a subtle linguistic joke: you start with "Sweet Business," and you finish with the "Bitter End". Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Bitter End - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Ionized Heatsink, Enhanced Heatsink, Accelerated Heatsink, Light Heatsink, Tactical Heatsink), (Attrition Orbs), (Frenzy)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. Bitter End is a Balanced Heat frame LMG, with a rapid-fire firing rate which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "good damage profile but mediocre total damage and worst recoil of any LMG frame". The heat frame offers built in Anti-Overload, and is like an extra perk set, first providing reduced heat generation, significantly increasing the number of shots before overheating, and providing a pseudo-Reconstruction, slowly "refilling" as the weapon loses heat when not being used. Additionally, there's an intrinsic ×1.15 damage boost against orange and yellow bars and vehicles, plus a ×2 vent rate if you manually reload when the heat bar is at >87.5% of maximum heat to proc Perfect Vent, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just left of vertical and pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the heatsink perk slot, perks that reduce Heat Generation or Vent Speed work best for this roll. For an all-purpose roll to tackle tougher adds, Attrition Orbs in the first trait perk column spawns a power orb after 45-50 hits within 5s of each other (so that's an orb every 3-4s) to proc armour mods, while Frenzy boosts Handling and Vent Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage. Aegis ranks the Imperial Allegiance origin trait as A-Tier - used in a fireteam, it stacks up to ×3 per ally within 15m, boosting cooling by a max ×1.105 and increasing damage when above 50% heat by max ×1.105. If you're interested, this is modelled on on the Z-6 rotary blaster cannon used by Hevy in the Clone Wars series. The name "bitter end" comes from old sailing ship terminology. The "bitts" are the heavy wooden or iron posts on the deck used to secure cables and ropes. The very last section of an anchor rope attached to the bitts is called the "bitter end." So, when an anchor rope was let out all the way to its absolute limit, a sailor reached the bitter end - meaning there was no rope left to give. It's also thought by the community to be a subtle linguistic joke: you start with "Sweet Business," and you finish with the "Bitter End". Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

Qullim's Terminus is a high-impact LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "highest total damage without losing that much DPS, also stagger intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. If you're interested, the lore isn't clear who Qullim was - possibly an Awoken, or a figure from a civilization destroyed by the Hive - who fell during past conflicts with Oryx, but the naming convention matches other King's Fall gear memorializing killed leaders and warriors. // Hammerhead - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Calber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Appended Mag, Light Mag), (Rampage), (Killing Tally, One For All, Desperate Measures, Onslaught)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Hammerhead is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. Hammerhead offers double damage perks for dealing with tougher adds, which is the usual go-to. In the first trait perk column, Rampage stacks up to ×3 on kills for ×1.331 damage for a refreshable 5s, with the stacks decaying one at a time. For your second column choice, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each, or Desperate Measures provides up to ×3 stacks for ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7.5s for a weapon and then two ability kills. Alternatively, Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 450 to around 720. There's two useful origin traits: Aegis ranks Forge's Kin as S-Tier - it provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, boosting the activation window by +2s each together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation and damage boosts of ×1.1 and ×1.13. There's also the B-Tier Indomitability which provides 6% grenade energy on Light subclasses or melee energy on Darkness subclasses, on kills. If you're interested, the name refers to the business-end of a blacksmith's hammer, a direct homage to the Black Armory's culture of industrial craftsmanship, forging weapons, and "striking down" humanity's enemies. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Hammerhead - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Calber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Appended Mag, Light Mag), (Fourth Time's the Charm), (Killing Tally, One For All, Desperate Measures, Onslaught)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Hammerhead is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the The recoil direction is vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. In the first trait perk column, Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your total ammo if you can keep hitting crits. For your second column choice for damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each, or Desperate Measures provides up to ×3 stacks for ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7.5s for a weapon and then two ability kills. Alternatively, Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 450 to around 720. There's two useful origin traits: Aegis ranks Forge's Kin as S-Tier - it provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, boosting the activation window by +2s each together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation and damage boosts of ×1.1 and ×1.13. There's also the B-Tier Indomitability which provides 6% grenade energy on Light subclasses or melee energy on Darkness subclasses, on kills. If you're interested, the name refers to the business-end of a blacksmith's hammer, a direct homage to the Black Armory's culture of industrial craftsmanship, forging weapons, and "striking down" humanity's enemies. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Hammerhead - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Calber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Appended Mag, Light Mag), (Rampage), (Killing Tally, One For All, Desperate Measures, Onslaught)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Hammerhead is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. Hammerhead offers double damage perks for dealing with tougher adds, which is the usual go-to. In the first trait perk column, Rampage stacks up to ×3 on kills for ×1.331 damage for a refreshable 5s, with the stacks decaying one at a time. For your second column choice, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each, or Desperate Measures provides up to ×3 stacks for ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7.5s for a weapon and then two ability kills. Alternatively, Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 450 to around 720. There's two useful origin traits: Aegis ranks Forge's Kin as S-Tier - it provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, boosting the activation window by +2s each together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation and damage boosts of ×1.1 and ×1.13. There's also the B-Tier Indomitability which provides 6% grenade energy on Light subclasses or melee energy on Darkness subclasses, on kills. If you're interested, the name refers to the business-end of a blacksmith's hammer, a direct homage to the Black Armory's culture of industrial craftsmanship, forging weapons, and "striking down" humanity's enemies. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Hammerhead - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Calber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Appended Mag, Light Mag), (Fourth Time's the Charm), (Killing Tally, One For All, Desperate Measures, Onslaught)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Hammerhead is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the The recoil direction is vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. In the first trait perk column, Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your total ammo if you can keep hitting crits. For your second column choice for damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each, or Desperate Measures provides up to ×3 stacks for ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7.5s for a weapon and then two ability kills. Alternatively, Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 450 to around 720. There's two useful origin traits: Aegis ranks Forge's Kin as S-Tier - it provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, boosting the activation window by +2s each together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation and damage boosts of ×1.1 and ×1.13. There's also the B-Tier Indomitability which provides 6% grenade energy on Light subclasses or melee energy on Darkness subclasses, on kills. If you're interested, the name refers to the business-end of a blacksmith's hammer, a direct homage to the Black Armory's culture of industrial craftsmanship, forging weapons, and "striking down" humanity's enemies. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Recurrent Impact - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Field Prep), (Headstone)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Recurrent Impact is a rapid fire LMG, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "good damage profile but mediocre total damage and worst recoil of any LMG frame". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left of vertical and loose so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are the best available. Recurrent Impact can be used for add clear with Stasis builds, perhaps supporting a double special loadout. For that, Field Prep in the first trait perk column grants +55 Reload Speed with a ×0.8 animation scalar to both reload and ready/stow when crouched, and bumps Ammo Generation by +40, which together with a Bandolier or Abundant Ammo mod pushes Ammo Generation to 80 or more. In the second column, Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill - these are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc Stasis subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Land Tank origin trait as S-Tier - it's strong for PvE, stacking up to ×3 on kills to provide +10 to your Health stat and a max ×0.85 damage resistance for 5s. If you're interested, the name doubles as a literal description of its rapid-fire function and a subtle nod to the psychological trauma of mind-altering psionic manipulation central to its narrative debut in Season of the Risen. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Chain of Command - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Calber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Appended Mag, Light Mag), (Adrenaline Junkie), (Desperate Measures, Killing Tally)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with grenade builds. Chain of Command is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is a little left of vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. Chain of Command offers double damage perks with refreshable timers that can be procced with grenades. Adrenaline Junkie, in the first trait perk column, stacks up to ×5 on kills for +20 handling and max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant max stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Pair that in the second column with either Desperate Measures for up to ×3 stacks for ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7.5s for a weapon and then two ability kills, or Killing Tally which gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, for a max combined damage boost of ×1.73. There's two useful origin traits - Aegis ranks the Vanguard Determination origin trait as B-Tier - providing up to ×3 stacks of damage resistance to max 6% - that's ×0.94 incoming damage - for a refreshable 12.5s, on kills within 20s of using a class ability. There's also the C-Tier Wild Card which provides the chance of Telesto-like "Experimental Submunitions" dropping on a kill - the number depends on the enemy tier - that last up to 10.5s and explode on contact or proximity to an enemy for additional damage. If you're interested, the name refers to the line of authority and responsibility within the military. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Chain of Command - PvE Utility god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Calber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Appended Mag, Light Mag), (Under-Over), (Desperate Measures, Killing Tally)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Champion PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE utility roll for dealing with barrier champions and boss Subjugators, or the Match Game modifier in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Chain of Command is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is a little left of vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, Armor-Piercing Rounds are best, but any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. Chain of Command offers the ability to rip through enemies with barriers and shields - even mismatched shields with Match Game or hidden ones like boss Subjugators - with Under-Over which provides ×1.55 damage against barriers and elemental shields, and ×2.4 damage against overshields. Pair that in the second column with either Desperate Measures for up to ×3 stacks for ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7.5s for a weapon and then two ability kills, or Killing Tally which gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, for additional damage. There's two useful origin traits - Aegis ranks the Vanguard Determination origin trait as B-Tier - providing up to ×3 stacks of damage resistance to max 6% - that's ×0.94 incoming damage - for a refreshable 12.5s, on kills within 20s of using a class ability. There's also the C-Tier Wild Card which provides the chance of Telesto-like "Experimental Submunitions" dropping on a kill - the number depends on the enemy tier - that last up to 10.5s and explode on contact or proximity to an enemy for additional damage. If you're interested, the name refers to the line of authority and responsibility within the military. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Seventh Seraph SAW - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Trickle Charge, Supercharged Magazine), (Rolling Storm, Jolting Feedback)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Arc builds. Seventh Seraph SAW is a high-impact LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "highest total damage without losing that much DPS, also stagger intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left but pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size work best for a boss damage roll. For sustained damage against bosses with Arc builds, in the first trait perk column Trickle Charge refills 10% of the magazine on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target - excellent sitting behind a Storm's Keep rally barricade - or Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity. In the second column for both target and AoE damage, Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 14 hits (11 when Amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning, or Rolling Storm generates ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks. Aegis ranks the Eyes Up origin trait as A-Tier, although the procs are a bit inconsistent, it grants up to ×15 stacks to improve Range, Reload Speed and boost damage by a max ×1.05 after a kill when in combat for a 8s, extendable to 25s on further kills. Otherwise, the C-Tier Rasputin's Arsenal refills 60% of your magazine on breaking an enemy shield or barrier. If you're interested, the name blends real-world military nomenclature (SAW stands for Squad Automatic Weapon) with Bungie's deep-rooted obsession with religious symbolism and the number seven. In Christian theology, Seraphim are the highest order of angels who sit directly at the throne of God, traditionally described as celestial beings of pure fire with six wings. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Seventh Seraph SAW - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Seraph Rounds, Tactical Mag Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Trickle Charge, Supercharged Magazine, Attrition Orbs), (Onslaught)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for perks for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Arc builds. Seventh Seraph SAW is a high-impact LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "highest total damage without losing that much DPS, also stagger intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left but pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, Seraph Rounds is the clear stand-out, otherwise perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are the best available. For taking on tougher adds with Arc builds, in the first trait perk column Trickle Charge refills 10% of the magazine on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target - excellent sitting behind a Storm's Keep rally barricade - or if you can keep mobile, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity. You could also leave the origin trait to help out with reloads, and use Attrition Orbs to spawn a power orb after 25 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods. In the second column, Onslaught doesn't increase total damage per magazine but, as you ramp up to the maximum 3 stacks, it does increase raw DPS for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 360 to around max 576. Aegis ranks the Eyes Up origin trait as A-Tier, although the procs are a bit inconsistent, it grants up to ×15 stacks to improve Range, Reload Speed and boost damage by a max ×1.05 after a kill when in combat for a 8s, extendable to 25s on further kills. Otherwise, the C-Tier Rasputin's Arsenal refills 60% of your magazine on breaking an enemy shield or barrierIf you're interested, the name blends real-world military nomenclature (SAW stands for Squad Automatic Weapon) with Bungie's deep-rooted obsession with religious symbolism and the number seven. In Christian theology, Seraphim are the highest order of angels who sit directly at the throne of God, traditionally described as celestial beings of pure fire with six wings. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Song of Ir Yût - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Jolting Feedback), (Kill Clip)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. Song of Ir Yût is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but no other benefits as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right but pretty tight so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed are pretty much essential for this roll if you're not using Hunter dodge to reload. For tackling add groups and the occasional tougher enemy, there's a double trait perk damage pairing in Jolting Feedback which applies Arc Jolt after 14 hits (11 when Amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits for both target and AoE damage, and Mega Kill Clip which boosts damage by ×1.4 with a duration of 3s + (2s × the number of kills) up to a max 20s, on a reload within 5s of a kill. Aegis ranks the Cursed Thrall origin trait as A-Tier -synergising well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee gives you 8.5s to proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion on your next weapon kill. If you're interested, "circular logic" is a psychological term for an argument that goes nowhere, looping back on its own unproven premises - which fits the messy, tangled web of thoughts and reflects the philosophy of Strand. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// 21% Delirium - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Eddy Current, Light Touch, Trickle Charge), (Gear Shift)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Arc builds. 21% Delirium is a rapid fire LMG, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "good damage profile but mediocre total damage and worst recoil of any LMG frame". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right and loose so you should improve recoil control or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. For dealing with tougher adds with an Arc build to proc Amplified, in the first trait perk column Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified, Light Touch boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and provides an ADS scalar while you're Amplified, or if you're using a Storm's Keep rally barricade, Trickle Charge refills 10% of the magazine on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target. In the second column for damage, Gear Shift stacks up to ×5 on reloads after kills or becoming Amplified for a refreshable 15s, granting up to a max ×1.35 damage boost. Aegis ranks the Gun and Run origin trait as D-Tier - it grants up to ×3 stacks on kills for 11s, which after sprinting for 0.75s consumes stacks to reload 25% of your magazine every 0.85s, refreshing the buff duration. If you're interested, the name is a play on atmospheric chemistry - Earth's atmosphere is ~21% oxygen - and the chaotic state of mind caused by intense combat. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Watchful Eye - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Overflow, Field Prep), (Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particular with Prismatic builds. Watchful Eye is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right and loose so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size work best for a boss damage roll. For a sustained damage roll with Prismatic builds, in the first trait perk column Overflow refills the magazine and boosts its capacity to ×2.2 of base, rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick. Alternatively, Field Prep grants +55 Reload Speed with a ×0.8 animation scalar to both reload and ready/stow when crouched, and bumps Ammo Generation by +40. In the second column for damage Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Aegis ranks the Willing Vessel origin trait as C-Tier - it generates up to ×5 stacks of a slowly decaying buff on dealing damage and kills, to boost Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name carries a deeper narrative meaning that ties directly into vanguard espionage, internal paranoia, and the monitoring of corrupted allies like Sloane during Episode: Heresy. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Watchful Eye - PvE Boss god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Overflow, Eddy Current, Field Prep), (Jolting Feedback, Rolling Storm)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particular with Arc builds. Watchful Eye is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right and loose so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size work best for a boss damage roll. For a sustained damage roll with Arc builds, in the first trait perk column Overflow refills the magazine and boosts its capacity to ×2.2 of base, rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick, or Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified. Alternatively, Field Prep grants +55 Reload Speed with a ×0.8 animation scalar to both reload and ready/stow when crouched, and bumps Ammo Generation by +40. In the second column for both target and AoE damage, Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 14 hits (11 when Amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits, or Rolling Storm generates ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 when Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks. Aegis ranks the Willing Vessel origin trait as C-Tier - it generates up to ×5 stacks of a slowly decaying buff on dealing damage and kills, to boost Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name carries a deeper narrative meaning that ties directly into vanguard espionage, internal paranoia, and the monitoring of corrupted allies during Episode: Heresy. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Retrofit Escapade - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Fourth Time's the Charm), (Target Lock)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Retrofit Escapade is a rapid fire LMG, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "good damage profile but mediocre total damage and worst recoil of any LMG frame". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is vertical but loose so you should improve recoil control or look to boost Stability with your barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, any of the combat effect mags, or perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are fine. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your total ammo if you can keep hitting crits. In the second column for damage, Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other for up to ×1.4 damage at 110.33% of base magazine capacity - so works well with FttC refilling the mag. Aegis ranks the Ambush origin trait as A-Tier - it improves both Range and Handling by +20 (+22 enhanced) and damage by ×1.1078 for 2s after first dealing or receiving damage. There's also the E-Tier for PvE Suros Synergy which grants +30 Handling and ×1.2 flinch resistance for 6s after reloading. If you're interested, the name carries a deeper thematic meaning that reflects the Season of the Seraph storyline, where players "retrofitted" old Warmind technology to avert an impending disaster. Literally, a retrofit means modifying an existing structure with new, updated parts, while an escapade refers to a wild, reckless adventure or an escape from a confining situation. Together, the name serves as a linguistic nod to the Tower's desperate, improvised mission: cobbled-together technology used in a high-stakes, chaotic operation to escape a looming apocalypse. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller)

// Unwavering Duty - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Fouth Time's the Charm, Envious Arsenal, Auto-Loading Holster), (Bair and Switch, Killing Tally, Target Lock)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Unwavering Duty is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just to the right of vertical and pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size work best for a boss damage roll. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your total ammo if you can keep hitting crits, or Envious Assassin overflows the magazine to up to ×3 capacity at 12.5% of base size per kill with primary weapons and 22.5% with specials before readying. Alternatively, Auto-Loading Holster refills the magazine 3.3s after stowing. In the second column, Bait and Switch boosts damage by ×1.3 for 11s for doing damage with your other two weapons, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed during an add phase up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, or Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other for up to ×1.4 damage at 110.33% of base magazine capacity - so works well with overflowed or auto-refilled magazines. There are three origin traits to choose from. Aegis rates the Roar of Battle as D-Tier for PvE - it grants +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s. Other options are the E-Tier Suros Synergy, which grants +30 Handling and ×1.2 flinch resistance for 6s after reloading, and the F-Tier Fleet Footed which stacks up to ×1 on hits and ×2 on kills to boost movement speed and Airborne Effectiveness for a refreshable 6s. If you're interested, the name has a direct, bittersweet deeper meaning rooted in the weapon's lore tab, reflecting the core thematic struggles of the Trials of Osiris team composed of Aisha, Reed-7, and Shayura, and represents the literal obligation and heavy psychological burden placed upon a Guardian’s shoulders, particularly when it comes to holding their traumatized allies accountable or protecting them. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Pro Memoria - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demolitionist), (Dragonfly, Frenzy)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Pro Memoria is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just to the right of vertical but pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size are the best available. To clear adds, perhaps supporting a double special loadout, in the first trait perk column Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, Dragonfly procs a 4.5m Strand explosion on a crit kill - this is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts and also procs weapon effects like Demolitionist, or Frenzy which, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s. Aegis ranks the Dealer's Choice origin trait as S-Tier - it provides an escalating bonus to super generation on a kill, depending on how many other Dealer's Choice weapons are equipped - this is the fastest way bar none to generating super energy. If you're interested, the name translates to "as a memorial" or "for a memory/ remembrance", and links to the ghost Targe reflecting on his bond with Zavala. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Pro Memoria - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demolitionist), (Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particular with Prismatic builds. Pro Memoria is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just to the right of vertical but pretty tight so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size work best for a boss damage roll. For a sustained damage roll, in the first trait perk column Demolitionist provides an instant magazine refill on grenade use and chunk grenade ability energy on kills. In the second column for damage Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Aegis ranks the Dealer's Choice origin trait as S-Tier - it provides an escalating bonus to super generation on a kill, depending on how many other Dealer's Choice weapons are equipped - this is the fastest way bar none to generating super energy. If you're interested, the name translates to "as a memorial" or "for a memory/ remembrance", and links to the ghost Targe reflecting on his bond with Zavala. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Corrective Measure - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist), (Elemental Honing, Aggregate Charge)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Prismatic builds. Corrective Measure is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just vertical and quite tight, so you can improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For sustained damage, in the first trait perk column Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the magazine empties, or Demolitionist provides an instant magazine refill on grenade use and chunk grenade ability energy on kills. In the second column for damage Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5, or Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by max ×1.33, with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. Aegis rates the Timelost Magazine origin trait as the best in game S-Tier - it provides an additional 1% super energy on kills and, after using your super, boosts magazine capacity ×2 for 21s - this stacks with trait perks that refill or boost mag size, like Overflow and Bolt Charge. If you're interested, a corrective measure is a mechanical or bureaucratic action taken to fix an error or bring a system back into compliance. Within the context of the Vault of Glass the name carries a sinister, clinical irony - the weapon is designed to forcibly "correct" the fabric of time and space by permanently deleting anomalous threats, acting as a tool of localised reality-editing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Corrective Measure - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Destabilizing Rounds, Dimensional Shift), (Demoralize)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Void builds. Corrective Measure is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just vertical and quite tight, so you can improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For add clearing on Void builds, in the first trait perk column Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s, or Dimensional Shift grants Dimensional Breach on picking up a Void Breach which, while active provides a refreshable 8s overshield on kills. In the second column, Demoralize spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken to nearby enemies for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 5s on a crit kill. Aegis rates the Timelost Magazine origin trait as the best in game S-Tier - it provides an additional 1% super energy on kills and, after using your super, boosts magazine capacity ×2 for 21s - this stacks with trait perks that refill or boost mag size, like Overflow and Bolt Charge. If you're interested, a corrective measure is a mechanical or bureaucratic action taken to fix an error or bring a system back into compliance. Within the context of the Vault of Glass the name carries a sinister, clinical irony - the weapon is designed to forcibly "correct" the fabric of time and space by permanently deleting anomalous threats, acting as a tool of localised reality-editing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroundAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist, Subsistence, Firefly, Destabilizing Rounds, Redirection), (Killing Tally, One For All, Demoralize, Butterfly)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Corrective Measure is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just vertical and quite tight, so you can improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For general add clearing, you have a range of magazine refilling perks in the first trait perk column - Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist and Subsistence - to choose from, as well as double damage with Firefly that also boosts reload, Destabilizing Rounds for Void volatility, or Redirection. Similarly, the second column has loads of damage-boosting options to pick from - Killing Tally, One For All, Demoralize and Butterfly all provide something to pair with your first column perks. Aegis rates the Timelost Magazine origin trait as the best in game S-Tier - it provides an additional 1% super energy on kills and, after using your super, boosts magazine capacity ×2 for 21s - this stacks with trait perks that refill or boost mag size, like Overflow and Bolt Charge. If you're interested, a corrective measure is a mechanical or bureaucratic action taken to fix an error or bring a system back into compliance. Within the context of the Vault of Glass the name carries a sinister, clinical irony - the weapon is designed to forcibly "correct" the fabric of time and space by permanently deleting anomalous threats, acting as a tool of localised reality-editing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Corrective Measure - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist), (Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, aperticularly with grenade builds. Corrective Measure is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just vertical and quite tight, so you can improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For grenade builds, there's the classic trait perk combination of Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use and Adrenaline Junkie which stacks up to ×5 on kills for +20 handling and max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis rates the Timelost Magazine origin trait as the best in game S-Tier - it provides an additional 1% super energy on kills and, after using your super, boosts magazine capacity ×2 for 21s - this stacks with trait perks that refill or boost mag size, like Overflow and Bolt Charge. If you're interested, a corrective measure is a mechanical or bureaucratic action taken to fix an error or bring a system back into compliance. Within the context of the Vault of Glass the name carries a sinister, clinical irony - the weapon is designed to forcibly "correct" the fabric of time and space by permanently deleting anomalous threats, acting as a tool of localised reality-editing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Corrective Measure (Timelost) - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist), (Elemental Honing, Aggregate Charge)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Prismatic builds. Corrective Measure is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just vertical and quite tight, so you can improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For sustained damage, in the first trait perk column Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the magazine empties, or Demolitionist provides an instant magazine refill on grenade use and chunk grenade ability energy on kills. In the second column for damage Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5, or Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by max ×1.33, with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. Aegis rates the Timelost Magazine origin trait as the best in game S-Tier - it provides an additional 1% super energy on kills and, after using your super, boosts magazine capacity ×2 for 21s - this stacks with trait perks that refill or boost mag size, like Overflow and Bolt Charge. If you're interested, a corrective measure is a mechanical or bureaucratic action taken to fix an error or bring a system back into compliance. Within the context of the Vault of Glass the name carries a sinister, clinical irony - the weapon is designed to forcibly "correct" the fabric of time and space by permanently deleting anomalous threats, acting as a tool of localised reality-editing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller
