// Corrective Measure (Timelost) - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Destabilizing Rounds, Dimensional Shift), (Demoralize)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Void builds. Corrective Measure is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just vertical and quite tight, so you can improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For add clearing on Void builds, in the first trait perk column Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s, or Dimensional Shift grants Dimensional Breach on picking up a Void Breach which, while active provides a refreshable 8s overshield on kills. In the second column, Demoralize spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken to nearby enemies for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 5s on a crit kill. Aegis rates the Timelost Magazine origin trait as the best in game S-Tier - it provides an additional 1% super energy on kills and, after using your super, boosts magazine capacity ×2 for 21s - this stacks with trait perks that refill or boost mag size, like Overflow and Bolt Charge. If you're interested, a corrective measure is a mechanical or bureaucratic action taken to fix an error or bring a system back into compliance. Within the context of the Vault of Glass the name carries a sinister, clinical irony - the weapon is designed to forcibly "correct" the fabric of time and space by permanently deleting anomalous threats, acting as a tool of localised reality-editing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroundAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Corrective Measure (Timelost) - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist, Subsistence, Firefly, Destabilizing Rounds, Redirection), (Killing Tally, One For All, Demoralize, Butterfly)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Corrective Measure is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just vertical and quite tight, so you can improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For general add clearing, you have a range of magazine refilling perks in the first trait perk column - Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist and Subsistence - to choose from, as well as double damage with Firefly that also boosts reload, Destabilizing Rounds for Void volatility, or Redirection. Similarly, the second column has loads of damage-boosting options to pick from - Killing Tally, One For All, Demoralize and Butterfly all provide something to pair with your first column perks. Aegis rates the Timelost Magazine origin trait as the best in game S-Tier - it provides an additional 1% super energy on kills and, after using your super, boosts magazine capacity ×2 for 21s - this stacks with trait perks that refill or boost mag size, like Overflow and Bolt Charge. If you're interested, a corrective measure is a mechanical or bureaucratic action taken to fix an error or bring a system back into compliance. Within the context of the Vault of Glass the name carries a sinister, clinical irony - the weapon is designed to forcibly "correct" the fabric of time and space by permanently deleting anomalous threats, acting as a tool of localised reality-editing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Corrective Measure (Timelost) - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist), (Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, aperticularly with grenade builds. Corrective Measure is an aggressive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "best balance of DPS and total amongst LMGs, stagger intrinsic good". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, but no other benefit as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just vertical and quite tight, so you can improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk, or just boost Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For grenade builds, there's the classic trait perk combination of Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use and Adrenaline Junkie which stacks up to ×5 on kills for +20 handling and max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis rates the Timelost Magazine origin trait as the best in game S-Tier - it provides an additional 1% super energy on kills and, after using your super, boosts magazine capacity ×2 for 21s - this stacks with trait perks that refill or boost mag size, like Overflow and Bolt Charge. If you're interested, a corrective measure is a mechanical or bureaucratic action taken to fix an error or bring a system back into compliance. Within the context of the Vault of Glass the name carries a sinister, clinical irony - the weapon is designed to forcibly "correct" the fabric of time and space by permanently deleting anomalous threats, acting as a tool of localised reality-editing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Bane of Sorrow - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Fourth Time's the Charm), (Aggregate Charge, Precision Instrument)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Bane of Sorrow is a high-impact LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "highest total damage without losing that much DPS, also stagger intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left of vertical and quite loose so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with your barrel perk, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best here. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your total ammo if you can keep hitting crits. In the second column for damage, Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by ×1.33, with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active or, as you're aiming for crits anyway with FttC, Precision Instrument grants up to ×6 stacks of escalating damage on repeated crit hits up to ×1.3 at 6 stacks for 1.25s or until you miss a crit. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, but the real impact is when it changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation, for double special builds. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Bane of Sorrow - PvE Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Fourth Time's the Charm), (Aggregate Charge, Precision Instrument)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Bane of Sorrow is a high-impact LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "highest total damage without losing that much DPS, also stagger intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left of vertical and quite loose so you could improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with your barrel perk, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best here. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your total ammo if you can keep hitting crits. In the second column for damage, Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by ×1.33, with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active or, as you're aiming for crits anyway with FttC, Precision Instrument grants up to ×6 stacks of escalating damage on repeated crit hits up to ×1.3 at 6 stacks for 1.25s or until you miss a crit. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, but the real impact is when it changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation, for double special builds. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Commemoration - Pve Boss god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Extended Mag, Appended Mag, Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Fourth Time's the Charm, Reconstruction), (Focused Fury, Firing Line)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Commemoration is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right but pretty tight, so you could still improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk anyway, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For sustained damage, in the first trait perk column Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your total ammo if you can keep hitting crits, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the magazine every 5.5s up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. In the second column for damage, Firing Line boosts crit damage by ×1.2 when you're within 15m of two or more allies in a fireteam, or Focused Fury boosts damage by ×1.25 for a refreshable 12s or until reload or stow, after hitting 35% of base capacity as precision hits - that's ~18. Aegis ranks the Bray Inheritance origin trait as B-Tier - it grants grenade, melee and class ability energy on hits, so works well for any ability build. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Commemoration - Pve Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Fourth Time's the Charm, Reconstruction, Subsistence), (Killing Tally, Rampage)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Commemoration is an adaptive LMG, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "technically a strict downgrade to aggressive damage profile, but barrier intrinsic is noticeable". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right but pretty tight, so you could still improve recoil control or look to boost Stability to help hit crits with the barrel perk anyway, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed work best for a boss damage roll. For add clearing, in the first trait perk column Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your total ammo if you can keep hitting crits, Reconstruction refills 25% of the magazine every 5.5s up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. In the second column for damage, Firing Line boosts crit damage by ×1.2 when you're within 15m of two or more allies in a fireteam, or Focused Fury boosts damage by ×1.25 for a refreshable 12s or until reload or stow, after hitting 35% of base capacity as precision hits - that's ~18. Aegis ranks the Bray Inheritance origin trait as B-Tier - it grants grenade, melee and class ability energy on hits, so works well for any ability build. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller
